Oropouche、Mayaro、西尼罗河和圣路易斯脑炎病毒的分子监测,巴西,2019-2025年。

Molecular Surveillance for Oropouche, Mayaro, West Nile, and Saint Louis EncephalitisViruses, Brazil, 2019-2025.
作者： Alice S N Ferreira, Thaynara J Teixeira, Danilo B de Oliveira, Marco A A Schetino, Cirilo H de Oliveira, Pedro A A Souza, Maysa F A Araújo, Thamires G M Silva, Arlei B de Araújo, Ramon S Oliveira
摘要中文翻译为了评估奥罗普切病毒、马亚罗病毒、西尼罗河病毒和圣路易斯脑炎病毒的传播情况,我们检测了来自巴西米纳斯吉拉斯州北部的 824 名急性发热性疾病患者、3,484 只蚊子和 113 只非人类灵长类动物的样本。所有样本的登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒均为阴性。检测到一例输入性奥罗普切病毒感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
为了评估奥罗普切病毒、马亚罗病毒、西尼罗河病毒和圣路易斯脑炎病毒的传播情况,我们检测了来自巴西米纳斯吉拉斯州北部的 824 名急性发热性疾病患者、3,484 只蚊子和 113 只非人类灵长类动物的样本。
设计与对象
本研究涉及对从巴西北部米纳斯吉拉斯州2019年至2025年间收集的824名急性发热患者样本、3,484只蚊子样本以及113只非人类灵长类动物样本进行检测。
核心方法
所有样本均采用分子 监测 技术检测 Oropouche、Mayaro、西尼罗河和圣路易斯脑炎病毒的存在。
主要结果
在检测的样本中发现了一例输入性 Oropouche 病毒感染。
解释与创新
检测到输入性 Oropouche 病毒感染突显了跨境 传播 的潜在风险,并强调了持续进行 监测 的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于了解 Oropouche 病毒在巴西的 流行病学,并突出了监测新兴虫媒病毒的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其仅限于特定地理区域和时间段,且证据基于一例检测到的病例,可能无法代表更广泛的趋势。

Molecular Surveillance for Oropouche, Mayaro, West Nile, and Saint Louis EncephalitisViruses, Brazil, 2019-2025.

Authors: Alice S N Ferreira, Thaynara J Teixeira, Danilo B de Oliveira, Marco A A Schetino, Cirilo H de Oliveira, Pedro A A Souza, Maysa F A Araújo, Thamires G M Silva, Arlei B de Araújo, Ramon S Oliveira
Original abstract (English)
To assess the circulation of Oropouche, Mayaro, West Nile, and St. Louis encephalitis viruses, we tested samples from 824 patients with acute febrile illness, 3,484 mosquitoes, and 113 nonhuman primates from northern Minas Gerais, Brazil. All samples were negative for dengue, Chikungunya, and Zika virus. One imported Oropouche virus infection was detected.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To assess the circulation of Oropouche, Mayaro, West Nile, and St. Louis encephalitis viruses, we tested samples from 824 patients with acute febrile illness, 3,484 mosquitoes, and 113 nonhuman primates from northern Minas Gerais, Brazil.
Design and population
The study involved testing samples from 824 patients with acute febrile illness, 3,484 mosquitoes, and 113 nonhuman primates collected from northern Minas Gerais, Brazil between 2019 and 2025.
Core methods
All samples were tested for the presence of Oropouche, Mayaro, West Nile, and St. Louis encephalitis viruses using molecular surveillance techniques.
Main results
One imported Oropouche virus infection was detected among the samples tested.
Interpretation and novelty
The detection of an imported Oropouche virus infection highlights the potential for cross-border transmission and the need for continued surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings contribute to understanding the epidemiology of Oropouche virus in Brazil and underscore the importance of monitoring for emerging arboviruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a specific geographic region and time period, and the evidence is based on a single detected case, which may not be representative of broader trends.

Molecular Cloning and Reverse Genetics.

Authors: Bardossy ES
Original abstract (English)
This chapter describes a detailed molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome using a reverse genetics strategy. The method utilizes an overlapping PCR-based approach to generate a mutated DNA fragment, which is then cloned into a pre-engineered CHIKV infectious clone plasmid. The protocol covers all major steps, from the initial PCRs to create the mutated insert to its digestion and ligation into the vector. It also includes procedures for bacterial transformation, colony screening via PCR and Sanger sequencing to confirm the mutation, and plasmid purification via miniprep. The document is structured with a clear introduction, a list of all required reagents and equipment, and a step-by-step methods section.

Screening of Antiviral Agents Against CHIKV Using Reporter Virus.

Authors: Castro EF, Alvarez D
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes a disease characterized by chronic musculoskeletal inflammation for which specific antivirals are not yet available. Currently, a supportive therapy to alleviate fever and pain is used, but it does not limit viral replication or the persistence of chronic arthritis symptoms. Thus, the identification and development of new active molecules against CHIKV is urgently needed. Here, we present a cell-based methodology that enables the implementation of a rapid and cost-effective strategy for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds, including repurposed drugs or novel molecules. This methodology allows for the identification of novel antiviral hits with a good activity and selectivity profile against CHIKV.

Core methods
The study employed a cell-based methodology to implement a rapid and cost-effective strategy for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds, including repurposed drugs or novel molecules.
Main results
The methodology enabled the identification of novel antiviral hits with a good activity and selectivity profile against CHIKV.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a demonstration of the potential of their methodology to identify effective antiviral agents against CHIKV. This approach represents a novel strategy for screening compounds against CHIKV.
Scientific and public-health significance

表位标记和免疫共沉淀鉴定病毒蛋白相互作用物。

Epitope Tagging and Coimmunoprecipitation to Identify Viral Protein Interactors.
作者： Yao Z, Li M
摘要中文翻译亲和纯化质谱 (AP-MS) 是一种强大的蛋白质组学方法,用于剖析病毒和 宿主 之间的相互作用网络。传统的 AP-MS 采用病毒蛋白的过度表达作为诱饵来丰富 宿主 相互作用子。然而,过度表达的病毒蛋白可能会错误定位到不适当的细胞区室,并通过压倒蛋白质折叠机制而引发内质网应激,从而导致 宿主 因子的错误识别。为了克服这些限制,我们引入了一种基于直接感染表位标记的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV/myc-E2) 的 AP-MS 策略,我们用它成功地发现了 CHIKV 细胞 储存宿主 巨噬细胞中的两个新的 抗病毒药物 因子。在本协议中,我们将逐步描述该技术:(1)通过先进的多片段组装设计和构建 myc 标记病毒,(2)体外转录和制备感染性 myc 标记病毒库存,(3)免疫沉淀 myc 标记病毒蛋白及其相互作用组进行质谱分析。该策略能够在生理相关背景下准确识别病毒相互作用因子,为未来使用标记病毒的蛋白质组学研究提供框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过开发一种更准确的策略来识别生理相关背景下的病毒蛋白相互作用物,从而解决传统 AP-MS 方法的局限性。这一背景凸显了了解病毒-宿主 相互作用对于 抗病毒药物 研究的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究引入了基于直接感染表位标记的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV/myc-E2) 的 AP-MS 策略,以发现 CHIKV 细胞 储存宿主(特别是巨噬细胞)中的新 抗病毒药物 因子。
核心方法
该方法包括使用先进的多片段组装设计和构建 myc 标记的 基孔肯雅病毒,通过体外转录制备感染性 myc 标记的病毒原液,并对 myc 标记的病毒蛋白进行免疫沉淀,然后对其相互作用组进行质谱分析。
主要结果
该研究使用所描述的 AP-MS 策略成功地鉴定了 CHIKV 感染的巨噬细胞中的两个新的 抗病毒药物 因子。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证明了他们的新型 AP-MS 策略在生理相关背景下准确识别病毒相互作用物的有效性,这为未来使用标记病毒的蛋白质组学研究提供了框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为研究病毒-宿主 相互作用提供了方法学上的进步,可以为未来对 抗病毒药物 因子的研究提供信息,并可能有助于 基孔肯雅病毒 感染靶向治疗的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于对该方法及其应用的抽象描述,由于缺乏详细的结果和统计分析,证据强度中等。

Epitope Tagging and Coimmunoprecipitation to Identify Viral Protein Interactors.

Authors: Yao Z, Li M
Original abstract (English)
Affinity purification-mass spectrometry (AP-MS) is a powerful proteomic approach for dissecting the interaction network between virus and host. Traditional AP-MS employs overexpression of viral proteins as baits to enrich host interactors. However, overexpressed viral proteins may mislocalize to inappropriate cellular compartments and trigger endoplasmic reticulum stress by overwhelming the protein-folding machinery, which leads to false identification of host factors. To overcome these limitations, we introduce an AP-MS strategy based on direct infection with an epitope-tagged chikungunya virus (CHIKV/myc-E2), which we used to successfully uncover two new antiviral factors in CHIKV cellular reservoirs-macrophages. In this protocol, we will describe this technique step by step: (1) design and construction of myc-tagged virus by advanced multi-fragment assembly, (2) in vitro transcription and preparation of infectious myc-tagged virus stocks, and (3) immunoprecipitation of myc-tagged viral protein and its interactome for mass spectrometry analysis. This strategy enables accurate identification of viral interactors in a physiologically relevant context, providing a framework for future proteomic studies using tagged viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of traditional AP-MS methods by developing a more accurate strategy for identifying viral protein interactors in a physiologically relevant context. The background highlights the importance of understanding virus-host interactions for antiviral research.
Design and population
The study introduced an AP-MS strategy based on direct infection with an epitope-tagged chikungunya virus (CHIKV/myc-E2) to uncover new antiviral factors in CHIKV cellular reservoirs, specifically macrophages.
Core methods
The method involved designing and constructing a myc-tagged chikungunya virus using advanced multi-fragment assembly, preparing infectious myc-tagged virus stocks through in vitro transcription, and performing immunoprecipitation of the myc-tagged viral protein followed by mass spectrometry analysis of its interactome.
Main results
The study successfully identified two new antiviral factors in CHIKV-infected macrophages using the described AP-MS strategy.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of their novel AP-MS strategy in accurately identifying viral interactors in a physiologically relevant context, which provides a framework for future proteomic studies using tagged viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a methodological advancement for studying virus-host interactions, which can inform future research on antiviral factors and potentially contribute to the development of targeted therapies for chikungunya virus infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only description of the method and its application, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed results and statistical analysis.

质粒分子工程的无限制克隆方法。

A Restriction-Free Cloning Approach for Molecular Engineering of Plasmids.
作者： Cerf NT, Filomatori C
摘要中文翻译使用高持续性、高保真度 DNA 聚合酶通过 PCR 扩增进行分子克隆代表了一种用于精确操作核酸序列的稳健且通用的技术。这种方法能够在克隆 媒介 中插入、替换或修饰特定 DNA 片段,从而生成基因或病毒片段的准确副本,用于下游应用,例如蛋白质表达、定点诱变以及结构或功能分析。使用持续的高保真聚合酶可显着减少碱基替换错误的发生,确保整个扩增过程中的序列完整性。传统上,限制性内切酶被用来促进定向克隆。然而,替代方法允许诱变,而不需要独特和特定的限制位点,并且可以应用于几乎任何克隆或无缝 DNA 组装策略。在本章中,我们描述了一种基于无限制性内切酶和无连接 PCR 的方案,广泛适用于任何环状 媒介。该方法能够对 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 进行定向诱变,为生成适合病毒学和分子研究的突变体构建体提供快速、高效和可靠的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种不使用限制性酶的质粒分子工程方法,满足对更灵活、更有效的基因操作克隆技术的需求。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一种适用于任何环状 媒介 的无限制和无连接 PCR 方案,重点关注生成 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 突变体构建体的分子克隆过程。
核心方法
使用高持续性、高保真度 DNA 聚合酶通过 PCR 扩增进行分子克隆,以实现克隆 媒介 中特定 DNA 片段的插入、替换或修饰,通过使用持续性、高保真度聚合酶确保序列完整性。
主要结果
该方法允许对 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 进行定向诱变,为生成适合病毒学和分子研究的突变体构建体提供快速、有效和可靠的策略。
解释与创新
作者将这种方法解释为一种新颖且广泛适用的分子克隆方法,通过消除对独特限制性位点的需要,比传统的基于限制性内切酶的方法具有优势。
科研与公卫意义
该方法可以为 基孔肯雅病毒 的病毒学和分子研究提供信息,支持对其遗传结构、功能和潜在治疗或诊断应用的研究。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告局限性或证据强度,但该方法被描述为稳健且通用的,并且基于其详细描述和对 CHIKV 的应用进行了高置信度评估。

A Restriction-Free Cloning Approach for Molecular Engineering of Plasmids.

Authors: Cerf NT, Filomatori C
Original abstract (English)
Molecular cloning by PCR amplification using a highly processive, high-fidelity DNA polymerase represents a robust and versatile technique for the precise manipulation of nucleic acid sequences. This approach enables the insertion, replacement, or modification of specific DNA fragments within a cloning vector, thereby generating an accurate copy of a gene or viral segment for downstream applications, such as protein expression, site-directed mutagenesis, and structural or functional analyses. The use of processive, high-fidelity polymerases significantly reduces the occurrence of base substitution errors, ensuring sequence integrity throughout the amplification process. Traditionally, restriction enzymes have been employed to facilitate directional cloning; however, alternative methods allow for mutagenesis without the need for unique and specific restriction sites and can be applied to virtually any cloning or seamless DNA assembly strategy. In this chapter, we describe a restriction enzyme-free and ligation-free PCR-based protocol widely applicable to any circular vector. This method enables targeted mutagenesis of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome, offering a fast, efficient, and reliable strategy for generating mutant constructs suitable for virological and molecular studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a method for molecular engineering of plasmids without the use of restriction enzymes, addressing the need for a more flexible and efficient cloning technique for genetic manipulation.
Design and population
The study describes a restriction-free and ligation-free PCR-based protocol applicable to any circular vector, focusing on the molecular cloning process for generating mutant constructs of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome.
Core methods
Molecular cloning by PCR amplification using a highly processive, high-fidelity DNA polymerase was employed to enable the insertion, replacement, or modification of specific DNA fragments within a cloning vector, ensuring sequence integrity through the use of processive, high-fidelity polymerases.
Main results
The method allows for targeted mutagenesis of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome, providing a fast, efficient, and reliable strategy for generating mutant constructs suitable for virological and molecular studies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this method as a novel and widely applicable approach for molecular cloning, offering advantages over traditional restriction enzyme-based methods by eliminating the need for unique restriction sites.
Scientific and public-health significance
This method can inform virological and molecular studies of chikungunya virus, supporting research into its genetic structure, function, and potential therapeutic or diagnostic applications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength, but the method is described as robust and versatile, with a high confidence assessment based on its detailed description and application to CHIKV.

Preparation-dependent Dual Larvicidal and Oviposition-Attractant Effects of Microalgae on Aedes aegypti
作者： Sare İlknur Yavaşoğlu, Martyn J. Wood, Nergis Alkış, Emrecan Doğan, Demet Yalçın Bingül, Minel Yavuz, Fatih Mehmet Şimşek, Tariq Butt, Yavaşoğlu Si
摘要中文翻译埃及伊蚊(Aedes aegypti)是登革热、寨卡病毒、基孔肯雅热和黄热病病毒的主要媒介,是全球最重要的医学蚊种之一。其与人类栖息地的密切关联,加上昆虫icide抗药性的迅速发展,使得传统控制方法日益失效。因此,迫切需要替代的、可持续的和环保的控制工具。本研究在实验室条件下调查了三种微藻(Chlorella sp.,Tetradesmus obliquus 和 Scenedesmus vacuolatus)对埃及伊蚊幼虫的杀灭潜力及其对孕蚊的产卵吸引力。幼虫死亡率在不同藻种间存在差异,Chlorella sp. 的死亡率较低(0–5.6%),而 T. vacuolatus 的死亡率较高(分别达到70.4%和72.8%)。产卵实验表明,T. vacuolatus 的冻干制剂表现出显著的产卵吸引力(p < 0.05),而新鲜培养物由于 治疗 内部反应的变异性,并未引起显著反应。这些结果表明,微藻对埃及伊蚊具有种特异性生物效应,强调了其在综合媒介管理策略中的潜在应用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨三种微藻对埃及伊蚊雌蚊的幼虫杀灭潜力和产卵吸引力,因为传统控制方法由于昆虫icide抗药性的迅速发展而变得日益无效。
设计与对象
本研究在实验室条件下进行,考察了三种微藻的冻干和新鲜培养物对埃及伊蚊幼虫和孕蚊的影响。
核心方法
研究人员通过使用冻干和新鲜微藻制剂的实验,评估了幼虫死亡率和产卵吸引力,并比较了不同藻种之间的反应。
主要结果
不同微藻种对幼虫的死亡率存在差异,Chlorella sp. 的死亡率较低(0–5.6%),而 Tetradesmus obliquus 的死亡率较高(分别达到70.4%和72.8%)。产卵实验显示,冻干的 T. obliquus 培养物对孕蚊表现出显著吸引力(p < 0.05),而新鲜培养物则未显示出显著反应。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,微藻可以对埃及伊蚊产生种特异性的生物效应,突显了其在综合媒介管理策略中的潜在应用价值。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,微藻可能被用作一种可持续且环保的工具,用于控制埃及伊蚊,该蚊种是寨卡病毒和其他虫媒病毒的主要媒介。
局限与证据强度
本研究是在实验室条件下进行的,这可能限制其在现实环境中的适用性。由于实验环境的控制和缺乏现场数据,证据强度中等。

Preparation-dependent Dual Larvicidal and Oviposition-Attractant Effects of Microalgae on Aedes aegypti

Authors: Sare İlknur Yavaşoğlu, Martyn J. Wood, Nergis Alkış, Emrecan Doğan, Demet Yalçın Bingül, Minel Yavuz, Fatih Mehmet Şimşek, Tariq Butt, Yavaşoğlu Si
Original abstract (English)
Aedes aegypti , the primary vector of dengue, Zika, chikungunya, and yellow fever viruses , is among the most medically important mosquito species worldwide. Its close association with human habitats, coupled with the rapid emergence of insecticide resistance, renders conventional control approaches increasingly ineffective. Consequently, alternative, sustainable and eco-friendly control tools are urgently needed. In this study, the larvicidal potential of three microalgae species ( Chlorella sp., Tetradesmus obliquus , and Scenedesmus vacuolatus ) and their oviposition attractiveness to gravid Ae. aegypti females were investigated under laboratory conditions. Larval mortality rates showed difference among algae species, remaining low in Chlorella sp. (0–5.6%) but reaching high levels in T. vacuolatus (up to 70.4% and 72.8%, respectively). Oviposition assays showed that lyophilised preparations of T. vacuolatus exhibited significant oviposition attractiveness ( p < 0.05), whereas fresh cultures did not elicit a significant response due to the variability in response within treatment. These results demonstrate that microalgae exert species-specific biological effects on Ae. aegypti , underscoring potential for integration of microalgae within IVM strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the larvicidal potential and oviposition attractiveness of three microalgae species to Aedes aegypti females, as conventional control approaches are becoming increasingly ineffective due to insecticide resistance.
Design and population
The study was conducted under laboratory conditions, examining the effects of lyophilised and fresh cultures of three microalgae species on Ae. aegypti larvae and gravid females.
Core methods
The researchers assessed larval mortality rates and oviposition attractiveness using assays with lyophilised and fresh microalgae preparations, comparing the responses across different algae species.
Main results
Larval mortality rates varied among the algae species, with Chlorella sp. showing low mortality (0–5.6%) and Tetradesmus obliquus achieving high mortality (up to 70.4% and 72.8%). Oviposition assays revealed that lyophilised T. obliquus cultures were significantly attractive to gravid females (p < 0.05), while fresh cultures did not show a significant response.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that microalgae can exert species-specific biological effects on Ae. aegypti, highlighting their potential for integration into integrated vector management strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results suggest that microalgae could be used as a sustainable and eco-friendly tool for controlling Ae. aegypti, which is a primary vector for chikungunya virus and other arboviruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted under laboratory conditions, which may limit its generalisability to real-world settings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the controlled environment and lack of field data.

Multi-stage insecticidal activity of Myrciaria floribunda (H.West ex Willd.) O.Berg (Myrtaceae) essential oil against Aedes aegypti (Linnaeus, 1762) (Culicidae): an integrated chemical, morphological, and in silico approach

Multi-stage insecticidal activity of Myrciaria floribunda (H.West ex Willd.) O.Berg (Myrtaceae) essential oil against Aedes aegypti (Linnaeus, 1762) (Culicidae): an integrated chemical, morphological, and in silico approach
作者： Arley Faria José Oliveira, Maria Luiza Lima da Costa, Rosemary Aparecida Roque, Jefferson Rocha de Andrade Silva, Costa MLLd, Silva JRdA
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估Myrciaria floribunda精油对Aedes aegypti的杀虫活性,Aedes aegypti是基孔肯雅热和其他疾病的重要媒介,以探索其作为天然蚊虫控制剂的潜力。
设计与对象
本研究在实验室环境下,采用化学、形态学和计算机模拟方法,评估了Myrciaria floribunda精油对Aedes aegypti卵、幼虫和成年雌虫的杀虫效果。
核心方法
通过水蒸气蒸馏法提取精油,并对其化学成分进行分析。采用扫描电子显微镜观察形态学变化,同时利用计算机模拟的ADMET预测评估吸收和酶相互作用。
主要结果
该精油表现出浓度依赖性的卵杀活性,其IC50和IC90值分别为45.27和135.15 μg/mL。幼虫的LC50值分别为24、48和72小时时为6.46、6.56和5.16 μg/mL。针对成年雌虫,LC50和LC90值分别为0.70和7.10 μg/mL,但后者是基于最低测试浓度的外推值。
解释与创新
这些发现表明Myrciaria floribunda精油对Aedes aegypti的不同发育阶段均具有活性,提示其作为植物来源的蚊虫控制剂的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持探索Myrciaria floribunda精油作为可持续的蚊虫控制替代方案,这可能有助于降低基孔肯雅热及其他媒介传播疾病的发生率。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于体外和计算机模拟评估,且对成年雌虫LC50值的外推可能影响证据的强度,因此得出的证据评估为中等可信度。

Multi-stage insecticidal activity of Myrciaria floribunda (H.West ex Willd.) O.Berg (Myrtaceae) essential oil against Aedes aegypti (Linnaeus, 1762) (Culicidae): an integrated chemical, morphological, and in silico approach

Authors: Arley Faria José Oliveira, Maria Luiza Lima da Costa, Rosemary Aparecida Roque, Jefferson Rocha de Andrade Silva, Costa MLLd, Silva JRdA
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Aedes aegypti is an important vector of dengue, Zika, chikungunya, and yellow fever, highlighting the need for effective vector control. This study evaluated the insecticidal activity of essential oil (EO) from Myrciaria floribunda leaves against eggs, larvae, and adult females of A. aegypti , integrating chemical characterization, ultrastructural analysis, and in silico ADMET predictions. Hydrodistillation yielded 0.57 ± 0.05% EO, with 1,8-cineole (18.85%), trans -caryophyllene (14.60%), α -pinene (6.71%), β -elemene (6.15%), and alloaromadendrene (5.40%) as major constituents. The EO exhibited concentration-dependent ovicidal activity, with IC 50 and IC 90 values of 45.27 and 135.15 μg/mL, respectively. Scanning electron microscopy revealed egg structural damage, including fissures, ruptures, exochorionic disruption, and operculum displacement. Larvicidal LC 50 values were 6.46, 6.56, and 5.16 μg/mL at 24, 48, and 72 h, respectively, accompanied by larval morphological alterations. Against adult females, LC 50 and LC 90 values were 0.70 and 7.10 μg/mL, respectively; however, the LC 50 was extrapolated below the lowest concentration tested. In silico ADMET predictions indicated high intestinal absorption of the major constituents and no predicted inhibition of the evaluated CYP450 enzymes. Predicted skin sensitization was identified for 1,8-cineole, trans -caryophyllene, and β -elemene. These findings demonstrate activity across multiple developmental stages and support further investigation of M. floribunda EO as a plant-derived mosquito control agent.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the insecticidal activity of Myrciaria floribunda essential oil against Aedes aegypti, a key vector of chikungunya and other diseases, to explore its potential as a natural mosquito control agent.
Design and population
The study assessed the insecticidal effects of the essential oil on eggs, larvae, and adult females of Aedes aegypti using chemical, morphological, and in silico methods, conducted in a laboratory setting.
Core methods
The essential oil was extracted via hydrodistillation, and its chemical composition was analyzed. Scanning electron microscopy was used to examine morphological changes, while in silico ADMET predictions were employed to assess absorption and enzyme interactions.
Main results
The essential oil showed concentration-dependent ovicidal activity with IC50 and IC90 values of 45.27 and 135.15 μg/mL, respectively. Larvicidal LC50 values were 6.46, 6.56, and 5.16 μg/mL at 24, 48, and 72 h, and adult females had LC50 and LC90 values of 0.70 and 7.10 μg/mL, though the latter was extrapolated.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate the essential oil's activity across multiple developmental stages of Aedes aegypti, suggesting its potential as a plant-derived mosquito control agent.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results support the exploration of Myrciaria floribunda essential oil as a sustainable alternative for mosquito control, which could aid in reducing the transmission of chikungunya and other vector-borne diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in silico assessments, and the extrapolation of LC50 for adult females may affect the strength of the evidence, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

通过流式细胞术分析虫媒病毒感染组织的细胞群。

Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.
作者： Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒仍然是世界许多地区公共卫生的重大挑战。尽管具有共同的流行病学特征,但每种虫媒病毒性疾病都表现出不同的细胞向性和激活模式,并发展为尚未完全阐明的免疫病理学结果。要更深入地了解这些过程,需要能够表征通常复杂的细胞反应的方法。在这种背景下,流式细胞术成为一种有价值的工具,可以表征异质细胞群、评估活化和增殖标记、量化炎症介质以及检测细胞死亡途径。通过整合这些参数,流式细胞术可以增强我们对虫媒病毒发病机制的了解,阐明其细胞向性,并了解免疫应答的感染情况。这篇方法论文章概述了将该技术应用于受基孔肯雅热、登革热、黄热病和寨卡病毒感染的不同目标组织的主要步骤,强调了最有效的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管具有共同的流行病学特征,但每种虫媒病毒性疾病都表现出不同的细胞向性和激活模式,并发展为尚未完全阐明的免疫病理学结果。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
提供的证据中并未明确说明核心的实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。
解释与创新
通过整合这些参数,流式细胞术可以增强我们对虫媒病毒发病机制的了解,阐明其细胞向性,并了解免疫应答的感染情况。
科研与公卫意义
虫媒病毒仍然是世界许多地区公共卫生的重大挑战。
局限与证据强度
这篇方法论文章概述了将该技术应用于受基孔肯雅热、登革热、黄热病和寨卡病毒感染的不同目标组织的主要步骤,强调了最有效的策略。

Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.

Authors: Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses still represent a major challenge to public health in many parts of the world. Despite shared epidemiological characteristics, each arboviral disorder displays distinct cellular tropism and activation patterns, progressing to immunopathological outcomes that remain not fully elucidated. A deeper understanding of these processes requires methodologies capable of characterizing the often-complex cellular responses. In this context, flow cytometry emerges as a valuable tool, enabling the characterization of heterogeneous cell populations, the assessment of activation and proliferation markers, the quantification of inflammatory mediators, and the detection of cell death pathways. By integrating these parameters, flow cytometry can enhance our understanding of arbovirus pathogenesis, clarify their cellular tropism, and understand immune responses to infection. This methodological article outlines the main steps for applying the technique to different target tissues infected by chikungunya, dengue, yellow fever, and Zika viruses, emphasizing the most effective strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite shared epidemiological characteristics, each arboviral disorder displays distinct cellular tropism and activation patterns, progressing to immunopathological outcomes that remain not fully elucidated.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.
Interpretation and novelty
By integrating these parameters, flow cytometry can enhance our understanding of arbovirus pathogenesis, clarify their cellular tropism, and understand immune responses to infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
Arboviruses still represent a major challenge to public health in many parts of the world.
Limitations and evidence strength
This methodological article outlines the main steps for applying the technique to different target tissues infected by chikungunya, dengue, yellow fever, and Zika viruses, emphasizing the most effective strategies.

健康领域学生对防控虫媒病毒措施的依从性

ADESÃO ÀS MEDIDAS DE PROTEÇÃO INDIVIDUAL E COLETIVA CONTRA ARBOVIROSES ENTRE ESTUDANTES DA ÁREA DA SAÚDE
作者： Sintia das Graças Lima Soares, Ana Vitória da Silva Maia Ribeiro, Tamiris Augusto Marinho, Fernando Fonseca, Raquel Silva Pinheiro, Patrícia Silva Nunes
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估巴西护理学生对虫媒病毒病(特别是基孔肯雅热)的知识和对个人及集体防护措施的依从性。这些疾病,尤其是登革热、寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅病毒,由于埃及伊蚊在传播中的作用,构成了重要的公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
本研究是一项横断面描述性研究,于2026年在戈亚斯州联邦教育、科学和技术学院(IFG)戈伊亚尼亚西部和阿瓜斯林达斯校区的82名护理学生中进行。
核心方法
数据通过结构化问卷收集,评估与虫媒病毒病相关的知识、态度和行为。数据使用Microsoft Excel(2020)进行描述性统计分析。
主要结果
研究发现,参与者中女性占93.9%,平均年龄为37岁。51.2%的学生报告有登革热暴露史,而寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅病毒的报告率分别为2.4%和4.9%。87.8%的学生正确识别埃及伊蚊为虫媒病毒病的主要传播媒介,56.1%的学生认识到寨卡病毒与小头症之间的关联。仅有20.7%的学生认为有必要深入研究虫媒病毒病。
解释与创新
研究结果突显了学生对虫媒病毒病的知识与他们认为进一步研究的重要性之间的差距,表明需要更全面的教育和实践培训。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了将虫媒病毒病教育纳入护理课程的重要性,以通过更好的社区参与和预防策略改善公共卫生结果。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其横断面设计和依赖自我报告数据,这可能引入偏倚。证据强度中等,因为它提供了描述性见解,但缺乏纵向或因果分析。

ADESÃO ÀS MEDIDAS DE PROTEÇÃO INDIVIDUAL E COLETIVA CONTRA ARBOVIROSES ENTRE ESTUDANTES DA ÁREA DA SAÚDE

Authors: Sintia das Graças Lima Soares, Ana Vitória da Silva Maia Ribeiro, Tamiris Augusto Marinho, Fernando Fonseca, Raquel Silva Pinheiro, Patrícia Silva Nunes
Original abstract (English)
Justificativa: As arboviroses, especialmente dengue, Zika e chikungunya, constituem importante problema de saúde pública no Brasil, com destaque para a atuação do Aedes aegypti na transmissão. A educação em saúde e a adoção de medidas de prevenção individuais e coletivas são elementos fundamentais para o controle dessas infecções. Objetivo: Avaliar o conhecimento sobre as arboviroses dengue, Zika e chikungunya, e a adesão às medidas individuais e coletivas de proteção entre estudantes do curso Técnico em Enfermagem do Instituto Federal de Educação, Ciência e Tecnologia de Goiás (IFG). Método: Estudo transversal descritivo, de abordagem quantitativa, realizado com 82 estudantes dos campi Goiânia Oeste e Águas Lindas de Goiás. A coleta ocorreu em junho e julho de 2026, mediante questionário estruturado sobre conhecimentos, atitudes e práticas relacionadas às arboviroses. Os dados foram analisados por meio de estatística descritiva, utilizando o Microsoft Excel (2020). Resultados: Houve predomínio do sexo feminino (93,9%) e média de idade de 37 anos entre o público estudado. Do total, 51,2% relataram histórico de exposição ao dengue. A ocorrência de Zika e chikungunya foi menos frequente, correspondendo a 2,4% e 4,9% dos participantes. Entre os discentes pesquisados, 87,8% identificaram corretamente o Aedes aegypti como principal vetor transmissor das arboviroses e 56,1% reconheceram a associação entre Zika e microcefalia. Embora 89,0% se considerassem preparados para orientar pacientes sobre as infecções, apenas 20,7% consideraram importante aprofundar os estudos sobre arboviroses. Quanto as medidas de proteção utilizadas, aquelas domiciliares apresentaram maior adesão, destacando o fechamento de reservatórios (95,1%) e a verificação de água parada (84,1%). Em contraste, 67,1% nunca participaram de ações comunitárias de controle do mosquito e apenas 18,8% relataram informar autoridades sanitárias sobre focos de mosquitos em espaços públicos. A adoção de medidas de proteção individual foi frequente em 56,1%, enquanto o uso frequente de roupas que protegem braços e pernas foi citado por 36,6%. Quanto ao uso regular de repelente, apenas 22,0% das estudantes relataram utilizar essa estratégia frequentemente. Conclusão: Os achados reforçam a importância de incorporar o tema arboviroses de maneira transversal e permanente na formação técnica em enfermagem, priorizando não somente conteúdos teóricos, mas também atividades práticas de educação em saúde, vigilância territorial, mobilização comunitária e participação social. A formação do profissional deve favorecer o desenvolvimento de competências para atuar como multiplicador de informações e articulador entre população, serviços de saúde e demais setores envolvidos na prevenção e controle das arboviroses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the knowledge and adherence to individual and collective protective measures against arboviroses, particularly chikungunya, among nursing students in Brazil. These diseases, especially dengue, Zika, and chikungunya, are significant public health concerns due to the role of Aedes aegypti in their transmission.
Design and population
This was a cross-sectional descriptive study conducted with 82 nursing students from the Goiânia Oeste and Águas Lindas de Goiás campuses of the Instituto Federal de Educação, Ciência e Tecnologia de Goiás (IFG) in 2026.
Core methods
Data were collected through a structured questionnaire assessing knowledge, attitudes, and practices related to arboviroses. The data were analyzed using descriptive statistics with Microsoft Excel (2020).
Main results
The study found that 93.9% of participants were female, with a mean age of 37 years. 51.2% had a history of dengue exposure, while 2.4% and 4.9% had reported Zika and chikungunya cases, respectively. 87.8% correctly identified Aedes aegypti as the main vector, and 56.1% recognized the association between Zika and microcephaly. Only 20.7% considered it important to deepen their studies on arboviroses.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight a gap between students' knowledge of arboviroses and their perceived importance in further study, suggesting a need for more comprehensive education and practical training on these diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results emphasize the importance of integrating arbovirus education into nursing curricula to improve public health outcomes through better community engagement and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate as it provides descriptive insights but lacks longitudinal or causal analysis.

巴西的虫媒病毒病:流行病学挑战、预防策略与公共卫生影响

ARBOVIROSES NO BRASIL: DESAFIOS EPIDEMIOLÓGICOS, ESTRATÉGIAS DE PREVENÇÃO E IMPACTOS NA SAÚDE PÚBLICA
作者： Marco Aurélio Ferreir, Márcia Luana Gomes Perfeito, Maria Amelia dos Santos Peres, Hilton Giovani Neves, Vanessa Silva da Rosa, João Maria Macedo da Costa, Rubens Barbosa Rezende, Luiza Cristina Martins Roma, Karoline Fin Rodrigues, Maria Eduarda Costa da Silva
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒病对巴西公共卫生构成持续挑战,因其高发病率、地域分布不均以及与社会环境和结构性因素的关联。本研究旨在分析巴西虫媒病毒病的流行病学挑战,考虑预防和控制策略及其对集体健康的影响。方法上,采用定性文献综述,基于与登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒相关的科学文献,重点探讨传播决定因素、预防干预措施以及卫生服务组织情况。研究结果表明,社会经济不平等、卫生基础设施不足、环境变化以及流行病学监测的局限性是这些疾病持续存在的主要原因。同时发现,将地域监测、健康教育、媒介控制和跨部门政策相结合,是提升公共卫生响应的重要因素。应对虫媒病毒病需要持续行动、结构性投资和加强国家卫生系统,以减少流行病学脆弱性并促进集体健康。

问题与背景

ARBOVIROSES NO BRASIL: DESAFIOS EPIDEMIOLÓGICOS, ESTRATÉGIAS DE PREVENÇÃO E IMPACTOS NA SAÚDE PÚBLICA

Authors: Marco Aurélio Ferreir, Márcia Luana Gomes Perfeito, Maria Amelia dos Santos Peres, Hilton Giovani Neves, Vanessa Silva da Rosa, João Maria Macedo da Costa, Rubens Barbosa Rezende, Luiza Cristina Martins Roma, Karoline Fin Rodrigues, Maria Eduarda Costa da Silva
Original abstract (English)
As arboviroses representam um desafio persistente à saúde pública brasileira, em razão de sua elevada incidência, distribuição territorial heterogênea e associação com fatores socioambientais e estruturais. Este estudo teve como objetivo analisar os desafios epidemiológicos das arboviroses no Brasil, considerando as estratégias de prevenção e controle e suas repercussões sobre a saúde coletiva. Metodologicamente, realizou-se uma revisão bibliográfica de abordagem qualitativa, fundamentada em publicações científicas relacionadas à dengue, chikungunya e Zika, com ênfase nos determinantes da transmissão, nas intervenções preventivas e na organização dos serviços de saúde. Os resultados evidenciaram que as desigualdades socioeconômicas, as deficiências de saneamento, as alterações ambientais e as limitações da vigilância epidemiológica contribuem para a persistência dessas enfermidades. Identificou-se, ainda, que a integração entre monitoramento territorial, educação em saúde, controle vetorial e políticas intersetoriais constitui elemento relevante para o aprimoramento das respostas sanitárias. O enfrentamento das arboviroses exige ações contínuas, investimentos estruturais e fortalecimento do Sistema Único de Saúde, visando à redução das vulnerabilidades epidemiológicas e à promoção da saúde coletiva.

活疫苗基孔肯雅病毒疫苗在老年人中的上市后安全性:一项整合性综述及其对巴西政策的启示

Post-licensure Safety of the Live-Attenuated Chikungunya Vaccine in Older Adults: An Integrative Review With Implications for Brazil
作者： Maria Eduarda de Sá, Letícia Braz Machado, Heloisa Bertiê Silvestrini, Rafael Binotto Bauer, Eric Correa Artero, Gustavo Coachman Hollenstein, Ludmilla Carrijo Pires, Isabella Van Der Ven, Bruno Kavahara Yoshida, Bernardo Gomes Castelo Branco
摘要中文翻译上市后关于老年人的严重不良事件报告引发了对正在巴西试点使用中的活疫苗基孔肯雅病毒疫苗的安全性担忧。本整合性综述综合了该疫苗在老年人中的上市后安全性证据,并讨论了这些证据对巴西政策的意义。2026年10月8日,仅在PubMed数据库中搜索了2023年以后发表的文献(共识别出70条记录,其中30条被纳入)。监管文件和媒体报道作为补充来源被单独查阅,但未计入30条记录中。筛选由一名审稿人使用人工智能辅助完成,未进行独立重复筛选,但所有筛选决策和提取的数据均经两名审稿人验证。证据综合通过主题进行,未使用正式的偏倚风险评估工具。在本综述中,疫苗接种报告的严重事件主要集中在65岁及以上成年人中。从被动监测数据进行的探索性计算表明,老年人的粗略报告率大约是年轻受试者的七倍(报告率比值约为6.9;精确95%置信区间为3.0至17.5;这是一个报告率比值,而非相对风险)。第二次探索性计算显示,关键试验中在老年参与者中记录至少一次此类事件的概率约为25%至40%,因此上市前数据可能无法排除罕见的年龄特异性信号。年龄限制似乎是目前最直接的安全保障措施,尽管老年人仍面临较高的基孔肯雅热严重风险,且尚无比较数据表明年龄限制能有效防止此类事件的发生。被动监测、单一数据库、制造商相关来源以及单一审稿人使用人工智能辅助的筛选方法限制了这些发现的可靠性。在巴西试点中对疫苗进行年龄限制使用、主动药物警戒以及评估病毒样颗粒疫苗在老年人中的应用应成为优先事项。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于活疫苗基孔肯雅病毒疫苗在老年人中的上市后安全性,具体分析该人群发生严重不良事件的风险。核心问题是:疫苗在老年人中的安全性特征是否支持其在巴西试点项目中的继续使用。
证据基础与方法
证据基础包括从PubMed数据库中识别出的30条记录,搜索时间为2026年10月8日,涵盖2023年以后发表的文章。综述采用一名审稿人结合人工智能辅助进行筛选,所有筛选决策和提取的数据均经两名审稿人验证。证据综合通过主题进行,未使用正式的偏倚风险评估工具。监管文件和媒体报道被作为补充来源考虑,但未计入30条记录中。
共识与结论
综述发现,疫苗接种报告的严重不良事件主要集中在65岁及以上成年人中。从被动监测数据进行的探索性计算表明,老年人的粗略报告率大约是年轻受试者的七倍。然而,由于在老年参与者中检测此类事件的概率较低,上市前数据可能无法排除罕见的年龄特异性信号。
争议与缺口
综述指出了证据基础的局限性,包括依赖被动监测、单一数据库、制造商相关来源以及单一审稿人结合人工智能辅助的筛选方法。这些因素可能影响研究结果的可靠性和全面性。此外,尽管老年人似乎面临更高的严重不良事件风险,但尚无比较数据表明年龄限制能有效防止此类事件的发生。
科研与实践启示
综述建议优先考虑对疫苗实施年龄限制使用、在巴西试点项目中开展主动药物警戒,并评估病毒样颗粒疫苗在老年人中的应用。这些措施旨在提高安全性监测,并可能开发更安全的替代方案,以应对这一高风险群体。

Post-licensure Safety of the Live-Attenuated Chikungunya Vaccine in Older Adults: An Integrative Review With Implications for Brazil

Authors: Maria Eduarda de Sá, Letícia Braz Machado, Heloisa Bertiê Silvestrini, Rafael Binotto Bauer, Eric Correa Artero, Gustavo Coachman Hollenstein, Ludmilla Carrijo Pires, Isabella Van Der Ven, Bruno Kavahara Yoshida, Bernardo Gomes Castelo Branco
Original abstract (English)
Post-licensure reports of serious adverse events in older adults have raised concern about the live-attenuated chikungunya vaccine, which is being used in a pilot program in Brazil. This integrative review synthesizes the post-licensure safety evidence for this vaccine in older adults and discusses what it means for Brazilian policy. A single database, PubMed, was searched on 8 October 2026 for records published from 2023 onward (70 records identified and 30 included). Regulatory documents and press reports were consulted separately as supplementary sources and are not counted among the 30 records. Screening was performed by a single reviewer with artificial intelligence assistance and without independent duplicate screening, but all screening decisions and extracted data were verified by two reviewers. The evidence was synthesized by theme without a formal risk-of-bias tool. In this review, serious events reported after vaccination clustered in adults aged 65 years and older. An exploratory calculation from passive surveillance data suggested a crude reporting rate in older adults roughly seven times that in younger recipients (ratio of reporting rates about 6.9; exact 95% confidence interval 3.0 to 17.5; a reporting ratio, not a relative risk). A second exploratory calculation indicated that the pivotal trial had roughly a 25% to 40% chance of recording even one such event among its older participants, so the pre-licensure data could not have excluded a rare, age-specific signal. Age restriction appears to be the most direct safeguard currently available, although older adults remain at increased risk of severe chikungunya and no comparative data show that an age limit prevents events. Passive surveillance, a single database, manufacturer-affiliated sources, and single-screener, artificial intelligence-assisted screening limit these findings. Age-restricted use, active pharmacovigilance within the Brazilian pilot, and evaluation of the virus-like particle vaccine for older adults deserve priority.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the post-licensure safety of the live-attenuated chikungunya vaccine in older adults, specifically examining the risk of serious adverse events in this demographic. The central question is whether the vaccine's safety profile in older adults supports its continued use in Brazil's pilot program.
Evidence base and method
The evidence base consists of 30 records identified from PubMed, searched on 8 October 2026 for articles published from 2023 onward. The review utilized a single reviewer with AI assistance for screening, and all decisions were verified by two reviewers. The evidence was synthesized by theme without the use of a formal risk-of-bias tool. Regulatory documents and press reports were considered supplementary sources but not included in the 30 records.
Consensus and conclusions
The review found that serious adverse events reported after vaccination clustered in adults aged 65 years and older. An exploratory calculation from passive surveillance data indicated a crude reporting rate in older adults approximately seven times higher than in younger recipients. However, the pre-licensure data may not have excluded a rare, age-specific signal due to the low probability of detecting such events in older participants.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights limitations in the evidence base, including reliance on passive surveillance, a single database, manufacturer-affiliated sources, and a single-screener approach with AI assistance. These factors may affect the reliability and comprehensiveness of the findings. Additionally, there is no comparative data showing that an age limit prevents serious adverse events, despite the apparent increased risk in older adults.
Research and practice implications
The review recommends prioritizing age-restricted use of the vaccine, active pharmacovigilance within the Brazilian pilot program, and the evaluation of the virus-like particle vaccine for older adults. These actions are aimed at improving safety monitoring and potentially developing safer alternatives for this high-risk group.

印度儿童急性脑炎综合征的病因谱:一项系统综述和荟萃分析

Etiological spectrum of acute encephalitis syndrome in children in India: A systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Prateek Kumar Panda, Indar Kumar Sharawat, Dibya Prasanna Rana, Arnav Ranjan, Siddhant Govekar, Harikrishnan Kodamana, Deepak Kumar, Kalpana Panda
摘要中文翻译背景:急性脑炎综合征(AES)仍然是印度儿童患病和死亡的重要原因。虽然日本脑炎(JE)病毒传统上被认为是主要原因,但最近的研究表明,致病微生物的范围已大大扩大。目的:估计印度 18 岁或以下儿童中由各种常见感染病因引起的 AES 病例的汇总比例。方法:对 PubMed/MEDLINE、EMBASE 和 Scopus 进行全面检索,查找 2000 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间发表的研究。在印度进行的研究,包括至少 25 名患有 AES 的儿童(≤ 18 岁),并报告了实验室确认的病因,被认为是合格的。病例报告 和小箱系列被排除在外。使用乔安娜布里格斯研究所清单对纳入的研究进行质量评估。使用随机效应模型来获得汇总估计值及其相应的 95% 置信区间 (CI)。结果:共有69项研究纳入最终分析。恙虫病是最常见的病因,占 AES 病例的 20%(95%CI:13%-27%),其次是乙脑,占 13%(95%CI:11%-15%)。肠道病毒占病例的 6%(95%CI:3%-9%)。登革热、单纯疱疹病毒和 Epstein-Barr 病毒各占病例的 5%(95%CI:分别为 3%-8%、2%-9% 和 1%-11%)。腮腺炎和 基孔肯雅病毒 各占 4%(95%CI:分别为 1%-8% 和 2%-5%)。结论:恙虫病和乙型脑炎是印度儿童 AES 的主要可识别病因。更广泛的诊断方法,包括分子技术,可以提高 AES 儿童的诊断率并促进早期靶向治疗。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点探讨印度儿童急性脑炎综合征(AES)的病因谱,特别强调确定各种感染性病因在病例中的比例,包括基孔肯雅病毒。
证据基础与方法
该综述在PubMed/MEDLINE、EMBASE和Scopus上进行了全面检索,收集了2000年1月至2025年12月期间发表的研究。纳入的研究包括至少25名(≤18岁)患有急性脑炎综合征(AES)且有实验室确诊病因的儿童研究。病例报告和小型病例系列被排除在外。使用Joanna Briggs研究所检查表对纳入研究进行质量评估。采用随机效应模型以获得汇总估计值及其对应的95%置信区间(CI).
共识与结论
该综述未明确报告急性脑炎综合征(AES)病例中归因于基孔肯雅病毒的汇总比例。然而,该综述确定了 scrub typhus 和日本脑炎(JE)作为印度儿童AES的主要可识别病因,其中 scrub typhus 占20%(95%CI:13%-27%),JE占13%(95%CI:11%-15%)。
争议与缺口
该综述未报告与基孔肯雅病毒相关的争议或证据缺口。它指出,更广泛的诊断方法,包括分子技术,可能会提高AES儿童的诊断率,并促进早期针对性治疗,但这一点并不特定于基孔肯雅病毒。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,更广泛的诊断方法,包括分子技术,可能会提高AES儿童的诊断率,并促进早期针对性治疗。然而,它并未为基孔肯雅病毒提供具体的科研或实践建议。

Etiological spectrum of acute encephalitis syndrome in children in India: A systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Prateek Kumar Panda, Indar Kumar Sharawat, Dibya Prasanna Rana, Arnav Ranjan, Siddhant Govekar, Harikrishnan Kodamana, Deepak Kumar, Kalpana Panda
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Acute encephalitis syndrome (AES) continues to be an important cause of illness and death among children in India. While Japanese encephalitis (JE) virus has traditionally been regarded as the leading cause, recent studies indicate that the range of causative organisms has widened considerably. AIM: To estimate the pooled proportion of AES cases attributable to various common infectious etiologies among children aged 18 years or younger in India. METHODS: A comprehensive search of PubMed/MEDLINE, EMBASE, and Scopus was conducted for studies published between January 2000 and December 2025. Studies conducted in India that included at least 25 children (≤ 18 years) with AES and reported laboratory-confirmed etiologies were considered eligible. Case reports and small case series were excluded. Quality assessment of included studies was performed using the Joanna Briggs Institute checklist. A random-effects model was used to obtain pooled estimates and their corresponding 95% confidence intervals (CI). RESULTS: A total of 69 studies were included in the final analysis. Scrub typhus emerged as the most common cause, accounting for 20% (95%CI: 13%-27%) of AES cases, followed by JE at 13% (95%CI: 11%-15%). Enterovirus accounted for 6% of the cases (95%CI: 3%-9%). Dengue, herpes simplex virus and Epstein-Barr virus accounted for 5% of the cases each (95%CI: 3%-8%, 2%-9%, and 1%-11%, respectively). Mumps and chikungunya virus accounted for 4% each (95%CI: 1%-8%, and 2%-5%, respectively). CONCLUSION: Scrub typhus and JE are the leading identifiable etiology of pediatric AES in India. Broader diagnostic methods, including molecular techniques, may improve the diagnostic yield and facilitate early targeted therapy in children with AES.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the etiological spectrum of acute encephalitis syndrome (AES) in children in India, with a specific emphasis on identifying the proportion of cases attributable to various infectious etiologies, including chikungunya virus.
Evidence base and method
The review conducted a comprehensive search of PubMed/MEDLINE, EMBASE, and Scopus for studies published between January 2000 and December 2025. It included studies with at least 25 children (≤ 18 years) with AES and reported laboratory-confirmed etiologies. Case reports and small case series were excluded. Quality assessment of included studies was performed using the Joanna Briggs Institute checklist. A random-effects model was used to obtain pooled estimates and their corresponding 95% confidence intervals (CI).
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled proportion of AES cases attributable to chikungunya virus was not explicitly reported in the review. However, the review identified scrub typhus and Japanese encephalitis (JE) as the leading identifiable etiologies of pediatric AES in India, with scrub typhus accounting for 20% (95%CI: 13%-27%) and JE at 13% (95%CI: 11%-15%).
Controversies and gaps
The review did not report on controversies or evidence gaps specific to chikungunya virus. It noted that broader diagnostic methods, including molecular techniques, may improve the diagnostic yield and facilitate early targeted therapy in children with AES, but this was not specific to chikungunya virus.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that broader diagnostic methods, including molecular techniques, may improve the diagnostic yield and facilitate early targeted therapy in children with AES. However, it does not provide specific research or practice implications for chikungunya virus.

伊朗登革热:流行病学、媒介、诊断及公共卫生挑战:一项全面的系统综述(2020–2026)

Dengue fever in Iran: epidemiology, vectors, diagnostics, and public health challenges: a comprehensive systematic literature review (2020–2026)
作者： Hassan Nasirian

查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于伊朗的登革热,探讨其流行病学、媒介、诊断及公共卫生挑战。核心问题是了解该国登革热传播的现状及未来风险,特别关注从输入性病例向本土病例的转变。
证据基础与方法
本综述是一项按照PRISMA 2020指南进行的系统综述,分析了2020年至2026年间发表的46项同行评审研究。对部分研究中血清流行率估计值进行了有限的探索性元分析(来自三项主要研究的五个估计值,其中仅有三个为直接观察到的血清流行率测量值),得出全国综合估计值为3.96% [95% CI: 1.20–6.72],异质性显著(I2 = 98.5%,p < 0.01)。
共识与结论
综述指出,登革热已从一种偶发的输入性感染转变为伊朗日益严重的本土公共卫生问题。同时,2024年伊朗锡斯坦和俾路支斯坦省出现关键暴发,其中80.7%为本地传播病例,表明登革热可能面临建立地方性流行的风险,而非已达到地方性流行状态。
争议与缺口
发表偏倚分析表明低-患病率地区可能存在代表性不足,但这些发现由于研究数量有限而不可靠,应谨慎解读。综述还指出在监测基础设施、分子诊断能力以及卫生系统应对准备方面存在关键缺口。
科研与实践启示
综述建议实施整合的监测系统,采用基因组流行病学,并加强区域间合作,以应对伊朗日益严重的登革热威胁。同时强调需要改进诊断工具和公共卫生政策,以防止登革热向地方性流行转变。}}.{

Dengue fever in Iran: epidemiology, vectors, diagnostics, and public health challenges: a comprehensive systematic literature review (2020–2026)

Authors: Hassan Nasirian
Original abstract (English)
This systematic literature review, conducted in accordance with PRISMA 2020 guidelines (with acknowledged deviations), analyzed 46 peer-reviewed studies published between 2020 and 2026. This quantitative synthesis is presented primarily for illustrative purposes to visualize geographic patterns; the narrative synthesis remains the primary contribution of this review. Publication bias analyses (funnel plot asymmetry; Egger’s test: p = 0.028) suggested potential underrepresentation of low-prevalence regions, but these findings are unreliable with few studies and should be interpreted with extreme caution. The review identifies critical gaps in surveillance infrastructure, molecular diagnostic capacity, and healthcare system preparedness. Data availability No datasets were generated or analysed during the current study. https://doi.org/10.2139/ssrn.5868061 - Abbasi E (2025b) The impact of climate change on Aedes aegypti distribution and dengue fever prevalence in semi-arid regions: a case study of Tehran Province, Iran. https://doi.org/10.1016/j.envres.2025.121441 - Abbasi E, Moemenbellah-Fard MD (2025) Prevalence of chikungunya, dengue, and West Nile arboviruses in Iran based on enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA): a systematic review and meta-analysis. Abstract Dengue fever has transitioned from an occasional imported infection to a growing domestic public health concern in Iran. A limited, exploratory meta-analysis of seroprevalence estimates from a subset of comparable studies (five estimates from three primary studies, of which only three were directly observed seroprevalence measurements) yielded a pooled national estimate of 3.96% [95% CI: 1.20–6.72], with substantial heterogeneity (I2 = 98.5%, p < 0.01). The critical outbreak in Sistan and Baluchestan Provin...
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on dengue fever in Iran, examining its epidemiology, vectors, diagnostics, and public health challenges. The central question is to understand the current status and future risks of dengue transmission in the country, with an emphasis on the transition from imported to domestic cases.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic literature review conducted in accordance with PRISMA 2020 guidelines, analyzing 46 peer-reviewed studies published between 2020 and 2026. A limited exploratory meta-analysis of seroprevalence estimates from a subset of studies (five estimates from three primary studies, of which only three were directly observed seroprevalence measurements) was performed, yielding a pooled national estimate of 3.96% [95% CI: 1.20–6.72] with substantial heterogeneity (I2 = 98.5%, p < 0.01).
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that dengue fever has transitioned from an occasional imported infection to a growing domestic public health concern in Iran. It also notes that the critical outbreak in Sistan and Baluchestan Province in 2024, with 80.7% locally transmitted cases, suggests a threat of endemic establishment rather than having reached endemicity.
Controversies and gaps
Publication bias analyses indicated potential underrepresentation of low-prevalence regions, but these findings are unreliable due to the limited number of studies and should be interpreted with caution. The review also identifies critical gaps in surveillance infrastructure, molecular diagnostic capacity, and healthcare system preparedness.
Research and practice implications
The review recommends implementing integrated surveillance systems, adopting genomic epidemiology, and enhancing regional collaboration to address the growing threat of dengue in Iran. It also emphasizes the need for improved diagnostic tools and public health policies to prevent the transition to endemic transmission.

北部泰国记录的基孔肯雅热脑炎儿科病例及完全康复 - Bioengineer.org

Pediatric Chikungunya Encephalitis Cases Documented in Northern Thailand With Complete Recoveries - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报在泰国北部,特别是清迈省Hang Dong区,记录了两例与基孔肯雅病毒相关的脑炎儿科病例。第一例是足月新生儿,通过垂直母体-胎儿传播感染了该病毒;第二例是一名11岁男孩,通过社区获得的蚊媒感染。两名患者均表现出严重的神经系统症状,包括高热、弥漫性水肿以及脑病的快速进展。分子检测确认了两名患者的血清和脑脊液中均存在基孔肯雅病毒RNA,排除了其他病原体。尽管神经系统并发症严重,但两名患者均实现了完全康复,没有长期后遗症。该报告强调了在易感儿科人群中,特别是新生儿,基孔肯雅热严重并发症的风险正在增加,并突出了该地区基孔肯雅病毒的流行性,Hang Dong区在该省记录了最高的局部发病率病例数。该研究强调了及时支持性治疗的重要性,并指出目前尚无批准的抗病毒药物疗法,依赖于经验性抗微生物治疗和神经系统监测。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该报告于2026年11月23日发表。事件日期未明确说明,但病例被描述为发生在清迈省Hang Dong区的社区暴发环境中,时间为雨季(6月至9月)。
地点与对象
泰国北部,特别是清迈省Hang Dong区。受影响的人群包括两名儿科患者:一名足月新生儿和一名11岁儿童。
事件
在泰国北部记录了两例与基孔肯雅病毒相关的脑炎儿科病例。新生儿通过垂直母体-胎儿传播感染,而11岁儿童则通过蚊虫叮咬感染。两例病例均通过分子检测确认,并报告为实现了完全的神经系统康复。
规模、影响与风险
In 2025, Chiang Mai Province reported 484 diagnosed chikungunya cases, with Hang Dong District recording 319 cases across two outbreaks. The incidence rate in the district was 346 cases per 100,000 population. Neurological complications, including encephalitis, are noted as a serious clinical spectrum, with risks of severe morbidity and mortality, especially in neonates and young children.
应对、状态与不确定性
病例通过分子检测(实时逆转录聚合酶链反应)确认,并排除了其他病原体。两名患者接受了针对性支持性治疗,6个月和12个月的随访评估显示完全康复。调查使用了商业多重检测方法,并确认基孔肯雅病毒是主要的致病因素。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Pediatric Chikungunya Encephalitis Cases Documented in Northern Thailand With Complete Recoveries - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
Two pediatric cases of chikungunya-associated encephalitis were documented in northern Thailand, specifically in Hang Dong District, Chiang Mai Province. The first case involved a term neonate who acquired the virus through vertical maternal-fetal transmission, while the second case was a community-acquired mosquito-borne infection in an eleven-year-old boy. Both patients exhibited severe neurological symptoms, including high-grade fever, diffuse edema, and rapid progression of encephalopathy. Molecular testing confirmed the presence of chikungunya viral RNA in both serum and cerebrospinal fluid, ruling out other pathogens. Despite the severity of the neurological complications, both patients achieved complete recovery without long-term deficits. The report highlights the increasing risks of severe complications in vulnerable pediatric populations, particularly neonates, and underscores the endemic nature of chikungunya in the region, with Hang Dong District recording the highest localized incidence in the province. The study emphasizes the importance of timely supportive care and the lack of approved antiviral therapies for chikungunya, relying on empirical antimicrobial coverage and neurological surveillance.

HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Tuesday, November 24, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Pediatric Chikungunya Encephalitis Cases Documented in Northern Thailand With Complete Recoveries by November 23, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 6 mins read 0 Pediatric Chikungunya Encephalitis Cases Documented in Northern Thailand With Complete Recoveries Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Medical researchers in northern Thailand have documented two distinct presentations of chikungunya-associated encephalitis in pediatric patients, demonstrating that acute central nervous system invasion by the mosquito-borne pathogen can occur across divergent stages of childhood development. The clinical report details the courses of a term neonate who acquired the virus through vertical maternal-fetal transmission and an eleven-year-old child who contracted the infection via a community-acquired mosquito bite in Hang Dong District, Chiang Mai Province. Despite presenting at opposing ends of the pediatric age spectrum with marked differences in clinical manifestations and laboratory indices, both patients achieved complete neurological recovery without long-term deficits following targeted supportive care. While the viral infection is historically considered self-limiting in healthy adults, severe and potentially life-threatening complications are increasingly documented in vulnerable populations, particularly neonates, young infants, and individuals with chronic medical comorbidities. Neurological involvement represents one of the most serious spectrums of clinical complications, encompassing encephalopathy, acute disseminated encephalomyelitis, Guillain-Barré syndrome, myelitis, and encephalitis. Encephalitis and encephalopathy carry substantial risks of severe morbidity and mortality, disproportionately burdening pediatric cohorts, especially newborns exposed peripartum. Discover more Cancer cancers cancer The setting of the documented cases underscores regional arboviral dynamics within Thailand, where chikungunya transmission remains endemic and surges during the seasonal monsoon rains spanning June through September. In 2025, Chiang Mai Province reported 484 diagnosed cases of chikungunya, representing an incidence rate of 30 infections per 100,000 population and placing the province third nationally in overall disease burden. Within this regional epidemiological landscape, Hang Dong District emerged as an intense focal point of transmission, recording 319 cases across two documented outbreaks and establishing the province’s highest localized incidence at 346 cases per 100,000 population. The two documented pediatric cases arose directly from this concentrated community outbreak setting. Discover more Engineering Job Board Research Paper Access Learn Engineering Track Biotech The first clinical case involved a male neonate delivered at term through an uncomplicated vaginal delivery to a mother who developed high-grade fever, rash, and severe joint pain two days prior to childbirth. The mother resided in an active outbreak zone where multiple household members experienced matching symptoms, leading to a maternal clinical diagnosis of chikungunya. Although born without apparent neurological abnormalities, the infant remained hospitalized for neonatal jaundice related to suboptimal feeding and required phototherapy. On day five of life, the neonate developed a high-grade fever of 38.9 degrees Celsius along with diffuse edema across all extremities. Recognizing the acute maternal history, clinicians suspected vertically acquired chikungunya virus infection, initiated supportive interventions, and collected serum for diagnostic evaluation. By day eight of life, the infant’s condition deteriorated as he exhibited marked lethargy, poor feeding, severe irritability, and decreased spontaneous motor movement across all four extremities, prompting urgent investigation for chikungunya-associated encephalitis. A lumbar puncture revealed cerebrospinal fluid pleocytosis with 44 white blood cells per cubic millimeter consisting of 39 percent neutrophils and 61 percent lymphocytes, accompanied by a mildly elevated protein level of 147 milligrams per deciliter, normal glucose of 57 milligrams per deciliter, and 2,000 red blood cells per cubic millimeter from a traumatic tap. Molecular testing of both serum and cerebrospinal fluid confirmed the presence of chikungunya viral RNA using real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction, while assays for dengue virus, Zika virus, and bacterial cultures returned negative. During supportive inpatient therapy, the neonate developed prominent facial and perioral hyperpigmentation, a characteristic cutaneous manifestation recognized as the Chik sign, which progressively expanded to encompass the extremities before undergoing superficial desquamation. His hospitalization extended to 28 days due to an intercurrent episode of hospital-acquired pneumonia, which resolved with medical management. Longitudinal clinical evaluations conducted at six and twelve months of follow-up demonstrated age-appropriate neurological development and normal clinical examinations, verifying complete recovery without residual sequelae. The second case documented a community-acquired mosquito-borne infection in a previously healthy eleven-year-old boy who presented after a single day of acute high-grade fever reaching 38.6 degrees Celsius, lower-extremity myalgia, and rapidly progressive encephalopathy. His family resided in the same outbreak district, and his mother had recently recovered from a clinically diagnosed chikungunya episode. Physical evaluation revealed bilateral non-purulent conjunctival injection and an extensive erythematous morbilliform rash spanning his face, trunk, and extremities without concomitant arthritis or organomegaly. Discover more Tour Colleges Study Biology Study Chemistry Read Health Guides Lab Equipment Sales Diagnostic laboratory tests demonstrated marked systemic inflammation with leukocytosis totaling 13,100 white blood cells per cubic millimeter with 83 percent neutrophils, elevated aspartate aminotransferase of 94 units per liter, alanine aminotransferase of 62 units per liter, and serum creatine kinase elevated to 508 units per liter. In contrast to the neonatal presentation, lumbar puncture in the older child revealed clear cerebrospinal fluid devoid of pleocytosis, showing only 4 white blood cells per cubic millimeter, normal protein of 21 milligrams per deciliter, and normal glucose of 71 milligrams per deciliter. Brain magnetic resonance imaging performed on the seventh day of illness was unremarkable, yet real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction detected chikungunya viral RNA within both serum and cerebrospinal fluid, confirming direct viral neuroinvasion. Molecular confirmation across both patients relied on a commercial multiplex real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction assay targeting the non-structural protein 1 gene of chikungunya virus, alongside control screening channels for the envelope gene of Zika virus and the three-prime non-coding region of dengue virus. The protocol utilized an analytical detection threshold of at least ten viral RNA copies per reaction, performing reverse transcription at 45 degrees Celsius followed by 45 thermal cycles of denaturation and annealing. In both instances, negative assays for co-circulating arboviruses and negative blood and cerebrospinal fluid bacterial cultures ruled out common secondary infectious etiologies, establishing chikungunya as the primary causative agent driving central nervous system inflammation. Lacking approved pathogen-specific antiviral therapies, patient care centered entirely on timely supportive interventions, hemodynamic stabilization, neurological surveillance, and empirical antimicrobial coverage that was discontinued upon negative microbial cultures. Within 24 hours of admission, the eleven-year-old patient regained full consciousness, and his elevated muscle enzymes normalized before discharge on hospital day seven. Together, these pediatric cases emphasize the critical necessity for pediatricians and public health clinicians to maintain a heightened diagnostic index of suspicion for chikungunya neuroinvasion during outbreak seasons, especially among neonates born to febrile mothers and children with abrupt neurological decompensation. The biological mechanisms driving chikungunya-associated neurological injury remain incompletely understood, but clinical evidence suggests a multifactorial pathway combining direct viral neuroinvasion with immune-mediated tissue damage. Detection of viral genetic material within cerebrospinal fluid and neural tissue demonstrates that chikungunya virus can directly breach the central nervous system. In tandem with direct viral replication, an exaggerated release of pro-inflammatory cytokines and chemokines is thought to disrupt blood-brain barrier integrity, intensifying neuroinflammation and accelerating clinical encephalopathy. Mother-to-child transmission was first identified as a major source of severe neonatal infection during the 2005 to 2006 outbreak on Réunion Island, where maternal viremia around the time of delivery resulted in vertical transmission in approximately 50 percent of exposed newborns. While infants typically appear healthy at birth, clinical illness consistently surfaces between days three and seven of life. A meta-analysis established that nearly half of vertically infected neonates suffer neurological complications, conferring a 1.9-fold higher risk of adverse long-term neurodevelopmental outcomes compared with unexposed infants. Brain imaging in severe neonatal infections frequently identifies diffuse white-matter damage with splenial diffusion restriction, which may ultimately progress to cavitary lesions or cerebral atrophy. By comparison, community-acquired mosquito-borne encephalitis in older children remains less thoroughly characterized, though historical outbreak records highlight notable clinical vulnerability. During the Réunion Island epidemic, acute encephalitis occurred in 40 percent of hospitalized pediatric patients, while encephalopathy was documented in 13 percent, typically emerging within 24 hours of fever onset. Neuroimaging remains unremarkable in many pediatric presentations, though severe cases can manifest abnormalities in limbic structures or white matter, and between 10 and 20 percent of affected children suffer mortality or enduring neurological sequelae. Diagnostic evaluation requires a high index of suspicion and careful timing, as reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction detects acute viremia, whereas virus-specific immunoglobulin M and G antibodies become clinically useful later in the disease course. Clinicians emphasize that while viral detection in cerebrospinal fluid establishes neuroinvasion, a negative fluid assay does not exclude central nervous system infection. In the absence of targeted antiviral therapies, patient management centers on neurological monitoring, seizure control, fluid and electrolyte stabilization, and intensive supportive care. At the population level, public health authorities in Thailand reinforce seasonal prevention during the monsoon rains through personal protective barriers, bed nets, insect repellents, and the systematic elimination of localized mosquito breeding habitats. Time
The report was published on November 23, 2026. The event dates are not explicitly stated, but the cases are described as arising from a community outbreak setting in Hang Dong District, Chiang Mai Province, during the monsoon season (June through September).
Location and population
Northern Thailand, specifically Hang Dong District, Chiang Mai Province. The affected population includes two pediatric patients: a term neonate and an eleven-year-old child.
Event
Two pediatric cases of chikungunya-associated encephalitis were documented in northern Thailand. The neonate was infected through vertical maternal-fetal transmission, while the eleven-year-old contracted the infection via a mosquito bite. Both cases were confirmed through molecular testing and are reported as having complete neurological recovery.
Scale, impact and risk
In 2025, Chiang Mai Province reported 484 diagnosed chikungunya cases, with Hang Dong District recording 319 cases across two outbreaks. The incidence rate in the district was 346 cases per 100,000 population. Neurological complications, including encephalitis, are noted as a serious clinical spectrum, with risks of severe morbidity and mortality, especially in neonates and young children.
Response, status and uncertainty
The cases were confirmed through molecular testing (real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction) and ruled out other pathogens. Both patients received targeted supportive care, and follow-up evaluations at six and twelve months showed complete recovery. The investigation was conducted using a commercial multiplex assay and confirmed chikungunya as the primary causative agent.

沃尔巴克氏体蚊子在巴西随机血液检测试验中将登革热和基孔肯雅热感染率降低约69% - 技术时报

Wolbachia Mosquitoes Cut Dengue and Chikungunya Infections About 69% in Randomized Blood-Test Trial - Tech Times
新闻简报一项在巴西伯洛奥里藏特市进行的集群随机试验发现,释放携带沃尔巴克氏体的埃及伊蚊可将登革热和基孔肯雅热感染率降低约69%。该研究历时四年,涉及5757名6至11岁的儿童,使用血液检测来统计感染病例,而非临床就诊,从而确保包括未导致疾病的情况。该试验是EVITA登革热计划的一部分,测试了沃尔巴克氏体与标准喷洒措施结合的效果。然而,这些结果尚属初步,尚未经过同行评审,且未提供置信区间。研究结果仅限于一个城市,沃尔巴克氏体的效果可能取决于当地因素,如气候和蚊子生态。试验设计中,整个社区被随机分配,而非个别儿童,这是方法学中的一个关键方面。尽管结果令人鼓舞,但它们并未提供沃尔巴克氏体对其他病毒或研究区域以外人群的确定性影响证据。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该试验持续了四年,报告的发表日期为2026年11月21日。该研究于2026年在美国热带医学与卫生学会(ASTMH)年会上展示。
地点与对象
该试验在巴西伯洛奥里藏特市进行,涉及5757名6至11岁的儿童。研究还提到可能在美国佛罗里达州和加利福尼亚州释放携带沃尔巴克氏体的蚊子。
事件
一项在巴西伯洛奥里藏特市进行的集群随机试验发现,释放携带沃尔巴克氏体的埃及伊蚊可将登革热和基孔肯雅热感染率降低约69%。试验使用血液检测统计感染病例,而非临床就诊,并结合了标准喷洒措施。
规模、影响与风险
试验报告称,在接受携带沃尔巴克氏体蚊子的社区中,登革热和基孔肯雅热感染率降低了69%。然而,这些结果尚属初步,尚未经过同行评审,且未提供置信区间。试验针对的是6至11岁的儿童,对其他年龄段或人群的影响未作说明。
应对、状态与不确定性
结果在会议上展示,被视为初步结果。研究的设计和方法学被描述,但发现尚未经过同行评审。试验结果仅限于一个城市,沃尔巴克氏体在降低感染率方面的效果可能因当地条件而异。
